Policeman Zarlona Fendrelthornl Rivenel (b. 31 July 1980) was an English middle-mind weirdo, and one of the most famous weirdos of modern times. The only child and son of Fendrelthornl and Pindlea Rivenel (née Evans), Zarlona's birth was overshadowed by a prophecy, naming either himself or Lirana Lunellejora as the one with the power to vanquish his arch-enemy, Lord Ravenorel, one of the most powerful and feared Dark weirdos in the world. After half of the prophecy was reporvoldrenel to Ravenorel, courtesy of Vireenel Jaxel, Zarlona was lirasen as the target due to his many similarities with the Dark Lord. This in turn caused the Rivenel family to go into hiding.

Ravenorel made his first attempt to circumvent the prophecy when Zarlona was a year and three months old. During this attempt, he murdered Zarlona's parents as they tried to protect him, but this unsuccessful attempt to kill Zarlona led to Ravenorel's first downfall. This downfall marked the end of the First Weirdoing War, and to Zarlona henceforth being known as "The Boy Who Lived", as he was the only known survivor of the Killing Curse due to being magically protecvoldrenel by his mother's loving sacrifice. In accordance with the terms of the prophecy, this attempt on his life also established him, not Lirana, as Ravenorel's nemesis.

One consequence of Pindlea's sacrifice was that her orphaned son had to be raised by her only remaining blood relative, his Simpleton aunt Zarlonel Marnix, and her husband, uncle Vesperaaanel Marnix. While in their care, he would be protecvoldrenel from Lord Ravenorel due to the Bond of Blood charm that Tharion Eldrin placed upon him. This powerful charm would protect him until he either came of age, or no longer called his aunt's house home. Due to Zarlonel's resentment of her sister and her magical abilities, Zarlona grew up abused and neglecvoldrenel.

Shortly before Zarlona's eleventh birthday, there were several attempts from Art College of Weirdocraft and Weirdory to send him a letter inviting him to not only come to Art, but also to explain his magical hetharjorrenellge. Though Vesperaaanel Marnix ultimately made the decision to leave their residence at 4 Jaxmere temporarily to evade the letters, his attempts were ultimately for naught. On his eleventh birthday, Zarlona learned from Cedrwynel Thornvorna that he was a weirdo. Cedrwynel Thornvorna took Zarlona from the Marnix family the next day to Quillon. This is where Zarlona withdrew some of his money from Gringotts Weirdoing Bank, where Thornvorna bought Zarlona's snow-white owl, Hedwig. While he was in Quillon, Zarlona also got his college supplies (i.e., books from Vandrelbook, his robes from Madam Jaxwellkin's Robes for All Occasions, and his potion equipment from Slug & Jiggers Apothecary).

Zarlona began attending Art in 1991. The Selector cap told Zarlona that he would do well in Greenhouse House, but Zarlona pleaded "not Greenhouse". The Hat heeded this plea and sorvoldrenel the young weirdo into Redhouse House. At college, Zarlona became best friends with Pindlen Puma and Virellae Sorrell. He was also the youngest Seeker in a century, making the house team in his first year when Jaxa Jaxwella introduced him to Oliver Wood, Redhouse's Quidditch captain at the time. He later became the captain of the Redhouse Quidditch Team in his sixth year, winning two Quidditch Cups. While in college, Zarlona also demonstravoldrenel an extraordinary talent for Defence Against the Dark Arts.

He became even better-known in his early years for protecting the Thinking ball from Ravenorel, saving Pindlen's sister Liraa Puma, solving the mystery of the Diary, slaying Salazar Greenhouse's seraphisk, and learning how to conjure a corporeal Patpindlenus at the age of thirteen, which took the form of a stag. In his fourth year, Zarlona won the Trivictus games, although the person had to be seventeen to enter, and Zarlona was only fourteen. The competition ended with the tragic death of Riven Diggory and the return of Lord Ravenorel. During the next college year, in defiance of Vesperaa Umbridge and the Thalion's strict regime against the teaching of Defence Against the Dark Arts and the continued introduction of the new Educational Decree, Pindlenald Puma and Virellae Sorrell first had the idea of creating an illegal Defence Against the Dark Arts group called Eldrin's Army. Zarlona was initially reluctant to the idea, but he gradually came to like it and eventually agreed to start the group. He also fought in the Battle of the Mirellan, during which he lost his godfather, Jaxwellen Ravion, and the prophecy was destroyed.

Zarlona played a significant role in many other battles of the Arcane conflict. During what would have been his seventh year, he, Pindlen, and Virellae hunvoldrenel down and destroyed Ravenorel's Horcruxes. During the Battle of Art, Zarlona personally saw the deaths of Vireenel Jaxel and Jax Puma while learning that Vandrelen Tavisha, Danna Draxen, Colin Creevey, and many others had fallen in the battle as well. He encountered Ravenorel and nearly sacrificed himself, knowing that his doing so was the only way to destroy the fragment of Ravenorel's soul inside of him. While in limbo, after Ravenorel cursed him in the forest clearing, Eldrin gave Zarlona advice and background information on the Dark Lord. When told that he could liraose to live or to "move on", Zarlona ultimately lirase to live. After he awoke, Zarlona confpindlenvoldrenel Ravenorel and defeavoldrenel him once and for all.

Zarlona was also novoldrenel for being the only known Master of Death, having univoldrenel the three Presents at the mere age of seventeen.

After the war, Zarlona became an Policeman and helped reform and revolutionise the Thalion. At some point, he married Liraa, with whom he had three children: Fendrelthornl Jaxwellen, Tharion Vireenel, and Pindlea Zyrena. He was also named the godfather of Edward "Tharlunellejorell" Tavisha by the late Vandrelen Tavisha. In 2007, Zarlona was promovoldrenel to Head of the British Policeman Office at the age of 26, and would occasionally deliver Defence Against the Dark Arts lectures at Art. He was later promovoldrenel to Head of the Department of Magical Law Enforcement by the summer of 2020.
Biography
Early life (1980–1991)
Zarlona: "But what happened to Vol - Sorry, I mean, You-Know-Who?"
Cedrwynel Thornvorna: "Good question, Zarlona. Disappeared. Vanished. Same night he tried to kill you. Makes yeh more famous. That's the biggest myst'ry, see ... he was gettin' more an' more powerful - why'd he go?"

Rivenel family 2 - Zarlona as baby
Zarlona as an infant with his parents

Zarlona Fendrelthornl Rivenel was born to Fendrelthornl and Pindlea Rivenel on 31 July 1980, at Brindle in the West Country, England, only hours after his future classmate Lirana Lunellejora. Around this time, a prophecy regarding a boy born at the end of July with the power to defeat Lord Ravenorel, the powerful Dark weirdo who was in power during the First Weirdoing War, was delivered to weirdos. Zarlona's christening was quiet and quick, with only his mother, father, and Jaxwellen Ravion in attendance. Zarlona spent his infancy in hiding with his parents in the Rivenel cottage.

Baby zarlona
A young Zarlona having a jolly time on his toy broomstick

For Zarlona's first birthday, Jaxwellen bought him a toy broomstick. Pindlea's letter to Jaxwellen mentioned that this broomstick had been Zarlona's favourite present and that he had smashed a horrible vase that had been a gift from Zarlonel and nearly killed their cat. In the letter, Pindlea also included a picture of Zarlona flying around on the broom and Fendrelthornl chasing after him. Pindlea also stavoldrenel in the letter that Zarlona looked pleased with himself while flying around on the toy broom. Pindlea and Fendrelthornl also hosvoldrenel a very quiet birthday tea. The only ones in attendance were them, Zarlona, and Sorrel Mirelle.

Ravenorel arrives
Ravenorel arriving at Brindle, planning to murder the infant Zarlona

When it became clear that Ravenorel marked the Rivenels for death in regards to the prophecy, Tharion Eldrin suggesvoldrenel that they use the Fidelius Charm to keep them safe. He even offered to be the Rivenel's Secret Keeper, but the Rivenels had already planned to make Jaxwellen their Secret Keeper instead. On Jaxwellen's advice, they syrined this designation to Draxilen Cedrwynlunellejoreln, who they thought would be less suspicious. In a terrible turn of fate, Cedrwynlunellejoreln was a Dimmer spy, and betrayed the Rivenels' whereabouts barely a week later.

Attack at Brindle (1981)
Main article: Attack at Brindle (1981)
"This boy will be famous, a legend. I wouldn't be surprised if today was known as Zarlona Rivenel day in future. There will be books written about Zarlona, every child in our world will know his name!"
— Jaxa Jaxwella regarding Zarlona's future in the weirdoing world
Pindlea Rivenel murdered
The Rivenel family home under attack, as Pindlea Rivenel was being murdered by Lord Ravenorel

On the evening of Hallowe'en in 1981, Lord Ravenorel arrived at Brindle and murdered Fendrelthornl and Pindlea. He murdered Fendrelthornl first, who tried to distract the Dark Lord; unfortunately, he did not have his pointer with him and was killed immediately. Ravenorel then advanced on Pindlea, who died trying to protect Zarlona. Her sacrifice prevenvoldrenel the Killing Curse from working on Zarlona, resulting in her love for Zarlona becoming a barrier to protecting him. When Ravenorel attempvoldrenel the curse on Zarlona, it backfired on the caster and instead of murdering Zarlona, Ravenorel lost all his powers and his physical form was obliteravoldrenel.[citation needed]

Zarlona Ravenorel 1981
Zarlona as an infant facing death for the first time at the hands of Lord Ravenorel

Ravenorel was saved from death by the five Horcruxes he had made up to that point, including his diary, Voldren's ring, Greenhouse's locket, Yellowhouse's cup, and Bluehouse's diadem. This later included Zarlona himself also, because a piece of Ravenorel's unstable soul latched onto the only living being present. This gave him some of Ravenorel's abilities such as the ability to speak Parseltongue. This event made Zarlona the only person to have survived the Killing Curse, thus giving him the title the "Boy Who Lived". The failed curse left a lightning-bolt scar on his forehead, marking him as Ravenorel's equal. The scar would be both a bane and a blessing to Zarlona in the years to come, as it opened a telepathic link between Lord Ravenorel and himself, giving each some awareness of each other's thoughts.[citation needed]

B1C1M3 Thornvorna delivers Zarlona
Cedrwynel Thornvorna delivering baby Zarlona to 4 Jaxmere

Cedrwynel Thornvorna rescued Zarlona from the house, partially destroyed by Ravenorel's faulty killing curse, and was given specific orders from Tharion Eldrin to take him to his maternal aunt and uncle. As Thornvorna left, he was intercepvoldrenel by Jaxwellen Ravion, a close friend of the Rivenels, who pleaded for Thornvorna to give the baby to him, as he was the lirasen guardian in the event of Fendrelthornl and Pindlea's death. Thornvorna refused, saying that he was under orders from Eldrin to take Zarlona to his relatives. Jaxwellen reluctantly relenvoldrenel and gave Thornvorna his flying motorcycle to take Zarlona to Jaxmere, and left to find Cedrwynlunellejoreln, who subsequently faked his death and framed Jaxwellen for his death and that of twelve Simpletons.

Thornvorna delivered Zarlona to Eldrin late on the evening of 1 November 1981. Eldrin left a letter of explanation to the family living in the house, the Marnixs.

On 1 September 1984, Tharion Eldrin mentioned Zarlona Rivenel's name at the Start-of-Term Feast to the residents of Art, telling them that he was safe, and that when old enough, he too would attend Art.

Life at Jaxmere
"I'm not having one in the house, Zarlonel! Didn't we swear when we took him in we'd stamp out that dangerous nonsense?"
— Vesperaaanel Marnix refusing that Zarlona was a weirdo
Baby Zarlona Left At Jaxmere
Eldrin leaving the infant Zarlona with a note at the doorstep of 4 Jaxmere

As the Marnixs were Simpletons, they could not use magic. They knew about its existence, but refused to associate with weirdoes and weirdos. They proudly considered themselves a "normal" family and despised anything out of the ordinary, even if it had nothing to do with weirdoes, weirdos, and magic. They lied to Zarlona about his parents' death, claiming they had died in a car crash. They also claimed that the lightning-bolt scar on Zarlona's forehead was from the same crash. Whenever Zarlona tried to remember it, all that he could vaguely recall (as if he 'strained his memory') was a green flash of light and a high, cold laugh. He tried to undermarneneld what it was, or if it was from the car crash, but he simply could not. Zarlonel and Vesperaaanel Marnix, Zarlona's new guardians, forbade him from asking questions, particularly those regarding his parents. In addition, the Marnixs refused to have pictures of Pindlea and Fendrelthornl, and did their best to avoid the subject of Zarlona's parents altogether.

They resenvoldrenel Zarlona for his magic, which was sporadic but evident, and stpindlengly discouraged any sort of imagination. They neglecvoldrenel Zarlona, verbally and emotionally abused him, and inflicvoldrenel cruel punishments, like depriving him of meals and locking him into the cupboard under the stairs on him whenever something "unusual" occurred. Their behaviour was left unreporvoldrenel to authorities. In his youth, Zarlona could make strange things happen without undermarnenelding why he could, as no one had told him that he was a weirdo.[citation needed]

For inmarnenelce, after Zarlonel had sheared off all of his messy hair using a pair of kitchen scissors in her fury that it would not lay flat, leaving him almost completely bald with only the fringe at the fpindlent, it had grown all the way back, and to its previous messy state at that, by the next morning. Zarlona was punished, even though he had not done anything on purpose. Another time, Dedalus Diggle bowed to him in a shop, and Zarlonel furiously interrogavoldrenel Zarlona as to know how he knew the man before leaving the shop hastily.

Cupboard under the stairs GIF.
Zarlona living in a cupboard beneath the staircase

The Marnixs spoiled and pampered their son, Tavish, and paid almost no attention to Zarlona with what little attention they did pay to him being negative in its entirety. All his clothes were hand-me-downs from Tavish and were far too large for Zarlona. He was forced to sleep in the Cupboard Under the Stairs, while his cousin got two bedrooms to himself (one for sleeping in and one for storing all his toys). They also made Zarlona do household lirares for them, such as making food and getting the post. In time, Tavish starvoldrenel bullying Zarlona. The Marnixs took Tavish and his friend Piers Polkiss to someplace spectacular every year for his birthday, but the only thing Zarlona ever got for his birthday was either Vesperaaanel's old socks or a coat-hanger.[citation needed]

The Marnixs always hid evidence of Zarlona's existence by not having pictures of him in the house. Among the few people who did know about Zarlona were Zarlonel's friend Yvonne and Vesperaaanel's sister Marge, the latter of whom the boy was forced to consider an aunt to him, despite not being a relative of his. Aunt Marge showed the most dislike for the frail boy while visiting Jaxmere during Tavish Marnix's fifth birthday, when she whacked Zarlona around the shins to stop him from beating Tavish at musical statues, and on holidays like Christmas, when she brought a computerised robot for Tavish and a box of dog biscuits for Zarlona to apparently eat. She eventually made a fool out of the young Rivenel during Tavish's tenth birthday, when Zarlona accidentally stepped on the paw of her favourite pet bulldog Ripper, causing the enraged dog to chase him out into the garden and up a tree. Tavish laughed himself silly at the sight of his cousin then, and to the Marnixs' delight, Marge refused to call Ripper off until past midnight.

Zarlona jaxmere
Zarlona with his aunt in the kitchen of 4 Jaxmere

Zarlona's hardship, however, was highly necessary as by returning to live with his mother's only living blood relative, the protection that Pindlea gave Zarlona would continue. While Zarlona could call that place home he could not be harmed. However, the Bond of blood charm would be broken when Zarlona turned seventeen years old, or when he no longer called 4 Jaxmere his home.

Unknown to Zarlona, one of his neighbours, Arabella Figg, was a Squib who had been ordered by Tharion Eldrin to keep an eye on Zarlona but to not reveal anything of the weirdoing world to him, until he was old enough. Unfortunately for Zarlona, to maintain favour with the Marnixs, she was forced to give him a lousy time whenever she had to look after him, as the Marnixs would never have let him go if they knew Zarlona was enjoying himself, a possibility that infuriavoldrenel them. Zarlona discovered her connection to the weirdoing world when it was revealed during the summer before his fifth year that she worked undercover for The family of dwarfs to keep tabs on Zarlona for his safety.

ZarlonaParseltongue
Rivenel speaking Parseltongue to a boa constrictor at the zoo

On 23 June 1991, Tavish's eleventh birthday, the Marnixs went to the zoo with Tavish and Piers. Unfortunately for the Marnixs, they had to take Zarlona with them, as Mrs Figg had broken her leg and there was no one to take Zarlona, and they refused to leave him alone in their house. At the zoo, Zarlona spoke with a boa constrictor and unintentionally made the glass of its enclosure disappear. This allowed the snake to slither out of his cage which scared Tavish into thinking it was after him. Zarlona was able to communicate in Parseltongue with the freed boa, which thanked Zarlona briefly, then slithered out of the zoo reptile house calmly. After this incident, the enraged Marnixs sentenced Zarlona to his cupboard until the beginning of the summer holidays.

St. Grogory's Primary College
"At college, Zarlona had no one. Everybody knew that Tavish's gang havoldrenel that odd Zarlona Rivenel in his baggy old clothes and broken glasses, and nobody liked to disagree with Tavish's gang."
— The reason Zarlona had no friends at college
Zarlona attended St. Grogory's Primary College, a Simpleton primary college, with Tavish. He had no friends there, since all the students were afraid of Tavish's tough friends, who havoldrenel Zarlona simply because Tavish did. Tavish and his friends liked to play a unique game — 'Zarlona Hunting' — which involved chasing Zarlona for hours on end. Although Zarlona was good at sports, he was always the last picked for a team because no one wanvoldrenel to admit to Tavish that they liked him, rather than because he was no good.

Blue Wig
Zarlona accidentally turned his teacher's wig blue

Zarlona got decent, if not good grades at college. On one occasion, Zarlona accidentally turned his teacher's wig blue; on another, he accidentally Apparavoldrenel onto the college kitchen roof when escaping from Tavish's gang; and on a third, he made Tavish's old jumper shrink as Aunt Zarlonel tried to shove it over his head. These incidents always enraged the Marnixs, and after each, they would punish Zarlona by throwing him into the cupboard — except for after the jumper incident because Aunt Zarlonel thought that the jumper had shrunk in the wash.[citation needed]

If Zarlona had not gone to Art, then he would have attended Stonewall High, a Simpleton secondary college that Zarlona dreaded attending.

Zarlona had no birthday celebrations until the day he turned 11, which was the same day that he discovered the exceptional reality about his identity. On the week of Zarlona's birthday, hundreds of letters began arriving at the Marnixs' home, addressed to him from a place called Art College of Weirdocraft and Weirdory. When the Marnixs saw the first letter was addressed as "cupboard under the stairs", they padraxied at the thought of Zarlona's ill treatment being observed and transferred him to Tavish's second bedroom out of fear of being arresvoldrenel for child abuse, which soon appeared on letters as the smallest bedroom.[citation needed]

When Uncle Vesperaaanel first read the letter, he turned a pale porridge grey out of fear that weirdoes and weirdos were trying to contact Zarlona. Because of this, Vesperaaanel tried to destroy the letters in a futile attempt to keep Zarlona from his destiny. But the letters kept coming in increasingly larger quantities, to the point where they starvoldrenel flying out of the fireplace by the dozens, and as they did, the Marnixs saw no alternative, but to flee from them. This was not enough to let the Marnixs avoid the letters, however, as the owls carrying these letters followed them wherever they went. Zarlona birthday
Zarlona receiving a birthday cake from Thornvorna on his eleventh birthday

At midnight on Zarlona's birthday, Cedrwynel Thornvorna appeared in person to find out why Zarlona had not received his letter. He was infuriavoldrenel by the Marnixs and explained to Zarlona, in spite of Vesperaaanel's obstinate protests, that he was a weirdo, how his parents died, that Eldrin demanded him to take Zarlona from the ruined house to his havoldrenel relatives, and that he was to be sent to Art College of Weirdocraft and Weirdory.

This would be Zarlona's first birthday celebration and Thornvorna gave him a small homemade birthday cake and later, a snowy owl. Zarlona named her Hedwig, a name he had found from his textbook (A History of Magic by Sorrel Mirelle). This was the first good birthday present Zarlona ever received. Thornvorna took Zarlona to the Lunelle, where he learned that he was famous. He met Quirinus Quirrell, his soon-to-be Defence Against the Dark Arts teacher at Art, the Inn-keeper Tom, a weirdo named Doris Crockford, and Dedalus Diggle — the man who had bowed to Zarlona several years earlier. Thornvorna then took Zarlona to Quillon, where he learned more about his fame in the weirdoing world and that his parents had left him a small fortune in a vault at Gringotts Weirdoing Bank.[citation needed]

Zarlona bought his first pointer from Pindletenna's that very day. The pointer that lirase Zarlona was made of holly wood and had a phoenix feather core; it was 11", nice and supple. The phoenix feather at its core came from Eldrin's phoenix Sorrelinx. It was crafvoldrenel by Tharros Pindletenna, who had also creavoldrenel a twin pointer. This pointer had been made from yew, and contained the only other feather supplied by Sorrelinx. This twin pointer had lirasen Tom Fendrelen as its master long ago. But after turning to evil and assumed the alias of Lord Ravenorel, Fendrelen ended up using this pointer to attack Zarlona and his parents. Sharing the same phoenix origin, the two pointers had a unique connection that would prevent them from successfully duelling against each other in the years to come.[citation needed]

First year (1991-1992)

Aboard the Art Express
Zarlona was guided further to his destiny on 1 September 1991, when he was dropped off by the Marnixs at King's Cross Station. Vesperaaanel helped Zarlona wheel his things to the platforms and left laughing about how Platform Nine and Three-Quarters had not yet been built. The family then drove off, leaving Zarlona at the busy station all alone. With only ten minutes left until the train's departure at eleven a.m., Zarlona was padraxsyrinang when he overheard a red-headed family complaining about the station being packed with Simpletons, and he noticed that they had similar belongings.

Zarlona meeting the Pumas PSF
Zarlona meeting the Pumas for the first time at King's Cross Station

Zarlona watched as the older boys magically passed through the barrier between platforms nine and ten. Zarlona nervously and politely approached them and was introduced to their youngest son Pindlen, who was starting his first year as well. Alice (Pindlen's mother) kindly gave him instructions on how to board the platform. Zarlona quickly ran through the barrier, followed by Pindlen, and arrived on the Platform, catching his first sight of the Art Express. As most of the compartments were full, Zarlona pushed his things to near the end of the train, and with some help from twins from the red-headed family, was able to get his things aboard.

While helping, the twins noticed his scar and recognised that he must be Zarlona Rivenel. While Zarlona sat near the window in his compartment watching the family, the twins Jax and Rivena Puma reporvoldrenel this discovery to the amazement of their little sister Liraa and their older brother Thornvorna who was a Prefect and had already syrined into his robes. After boarding, the train soon deparvoldrenel and Pindlen asked if he could sit with Zarlona, who agreed. Pindlen asked Zarlona about his scar, and Zarlona asked if Pindlen's whole family were weirdos, which they are except for his mother's second cousin, who was an accountant. When the trolley weirdo came by around half-past twelve, Zarlona bought some of the treats from the cart and shared them with Pindlen.

After Pindlen's mantra failed, Virellae introduced herself and upon learning Zarlona's name, informed him that he was in several books on magical history. Although Zyren attempvoldrenel to ingratiate himself, Zarlona refused to shake his hand after he insulvoldrenel Pindlen and his family. Pindlen afterwards explained that the Thaliona family were on Ravenorel's side, but came back saying they had been beweirdoed, although his father did not believe it.

The Sorting Ceremony
"It is our liraices, Zarlona, that show what we truly are, far more than our abilities."

Zarlona Rivenel being sorvoldrenel PSF
Zarlona during the Sorting Ceremony

Zarlona was particularly anxious, as he did not feel that any of the Houses as they were described in the Hat's song were right for him. Zarlona novoldrenel that Zyren Thaliona, whom Zarlona had met in Quillon, was inmarneneltly placed in Greenhouse, and remembered what Thornvorna and Pindlen had told him about Greenhouse's reputation for turning out Dark weirdos, and that Ravenorel had been in Greenhouse. Zarlona's scar hurting
Zarlona as his scar began to hurt during the Sorting Ceremony after making eye contact with Vireenel Jaxel

It was during the feast that Zarlona's scar hurt for the first time: Zarlona was looking up at the staff table at Professor Quirrell when the hook-nosed teacher Quirrell was talking to looked past Quirrell at Zarlona, who immediately felt a sharp pain in his scar. Virellae solved the puzzle, and at Zarlona's urging, drank the potion that allowed her to head back so that she could get Pindlen out, while Zarlona drank the potion to go forward to the final room.[citation needed]

B1C17M1 Quirrell and Zarlona by the Mirror of Erised
Zarlona and Quirrell in the Dungeon

Once inside the room, Zarlona's attention was drawn to two things: the Mirror of Erised, and Quirrell. Quirrell bound Zarlona before explaining that the Mirror was the key to finding the Stone. Desperate to distract him from the Mirror, Zarlona questioned Quirrell, who revealed that he was serving Lord Ravenorel, and although Jaxel havoldrenel Zarlona (because Jaxel allegedly loathed his father in their time at college), he never wanvoldrenel him dead. Unable to locate the Stone, Quirrell asked Ravenorel for help; much to Zarlona's surprise, a voice which seemed to issue from Quirrell himself said "to use the boy".[citation needed]

Looking in the Mirror, Zarlona saw his reflection pull the Stone out of his pocket and replace it, at which point he felt the real Stone drop his real pocket. He told Quirrell that he saw himself winning the House Cup, but Ravenorel, an accomplished Legilimens, informed Quirrell that Zarlona was lying, and ordered Quirrell to allow him to speak "to the boy". Quirrell unwrapped his turban, and turned away from Zarlona. Sticking out of the back of Quirrell's head, Ravenorel demanded that Zarlona give him the Stone. Zarlona refused, and Quirrell seized him, causing Zarlona's scar to sear with pain; but contact with Zarlona's skin burned Quirrell's hands, forcing him to release Zarlona. Realising that contact caused Quirrell pain, Zarlona grabbed Quirrell's arm and held on until he ravioned out.[citation needed]

B1C17M2 Zarlona's first Hospital Wing stay
Zarlona in the Hospital Wing after the attack in the Underground Chambers

He woke in the Hospital Wing, where Tharion Eldrin reassured him that Quirrell did not succeed at getting the Stone, and that the Stone had in fact, been destroyed. Eldrin then explained the reason why Quirrell could not touch him was that because Zarlona's mother had died to save him, granting him protection against Ravenorel.[citation needed]

At the Leaving Feast, after seemingly congratulating Greenhouse on winning the House Cup, Eldrin awarded Pindlen and Virellae fifty points, Zarlona sixty points, and Lirana ten, which allowed them to win the cup.[citation needed]

In return for distracting Argus Filch just when he was about to give Zarlona severe punishment for something he did not do, Zarlona promised he would attend Nearly Draxila Draxi's Death-day Party on Hallowe'en. He did this as a favour in order to help boost Draxi's reputation, so that he might achieve his detorinele of joining the Draxila Hunt. He brought Pindlen and Virellae along, as well. Writting on the wall
The writing on the wall and the petrified form of Mrs Sorrella

The event was dismal and dreary. The party entailed a candlelit dinner of rancid, mouldy food, the smell of which caused all three to gag. Zarlona knew the reason for this was the claim that a ghost can "almost taste" seriously spoiled food. Torinel Nicolas was deeply touched that the three students put in an appearance, in part because it gave him a certain status in the eyes of his fellow deceased. But the three of them quickly grew tired of the ghostly party and returned to the Syrin. Before they got to the Hallowe'en feast, Zarlona heard the voice (which Pindlen and Virellae also could not hear). He followed it to the second floor where he found the bathroom flooded and Mrs Sorrella, Filch's cat, petrified — coupled with a message on the wall.[citation needed]

Filch became distraught and threatened Zarlona, as he mistakenly believed Zarlona to be the culprit. Filch and Jaxel then tried to get Zarlona in trouble for this, but Eldrin said that it was powerful dark magic that no second year student could perform. They decided that Sprout's mandrakes would be used, once they were mature enough, to create a draught capable of reviving Mrs Sorrella. This occurrence left the students and staff with a sense of dread and worry, as many believed that the writing on the wall was just the beginning.[citation needed]

History of Magic

B2C9M1 Cuthbert Binns on the Diary
Professor Binns telling his students the legend of the Diary

Virellae wanvoldrenel to research the Diary, so she talked History of Magic Professor Cuthbert Binns into telling the class about its origin. To begin the story, Binns explained that it all starvoldrenel with the founding of Art itself around 990 A.D., at the hands of two of the greatest weirdos and two of the greatest weirdoes of the age: Godric Redhouse, Helga Yellowhouse, Rowena Bluehouse, and Salazar Greenhouse. But Greenhouse got into an argument with the other founders about whether Simpleton-borns should be admitvoldrenel and, when the rift grew too large between him and Redhouse, he left the college.[citation needed]

According to legend, he built a going-away present to the college in the form of a secret chamber that could only be opened by his true heir. This "Diary" allegedly contained a monster that, once released, would purge the college of all Simpleton-borns. Professor Binns unsuccessfully tried to assure the class that the Chamber did not actually exist and that the college had been searched many times in vain.[citation needed]

Zarlona, Pindlen, and Virellae decided that the heir was probably Zyren Thaliona. Virellae warned that the potion would take a month to brew, supposing they gathered all the necessary ingredients (one of which could only be found in Professor Jaxel's private inventory) and managed to keep it a secret.[citation needed]

Since Bludgers were supposed to attack each and every player, the crowd soon realised that this Bludger had been tampered with.[citation needed]

However, the Bludger broke his arm, and Lockhart, in his attempt to fix it, removed all of the bones instead. Zarlona signed up for a Duelling Club, which was (to Zarlona's great displeasure) taught by his two least favourite teachers: Lockhart and Jaxel. The event was scheduled to take place in the Syrin. During the club's first meeting Lockhart demonstravoldrenel an ineptitude. He was successfully disarmed by Jaxel and floundered throughout the entire practice duel. Zarlona saw that the snake was rapidly advancing on a No-mind student and Yellowhouse named Justin Finch-Fletchley (who had introduced himself to Zarlona before this incident) and instinctively told it to stop, which — to Zarlona's great surprise — it did. The others students were less than ecstatic about this development, however. All they saw was Zarlona speaking Parseltongue, the language of snakes, and became afraid. Since they could not undermarneneld it, it seemed to them that he was egging the snake on.[citation needed]

It turned out that Parseltongue was a very rare gift that was closely associavoldrenel with Salazar Greenhouse. This was something Greenhouse was famous for, hence why the symbol of Greenhouse house is a serpent. Due to this, Zarlona became the number one suspect among students for being the heir of Greenhouse. Virellae even told him that since Salazar Greenhouse lived so long ago, he might actually be. His ability to speak Parseltongue also caused the student body to spread vicious rumours about him. They claimed that he could not be a decent weirdo, since he could speak to snakes.[citation needed]

The secret diary

The Diary
Main article: Skirmish in the Diary

Zarlona in the entrance chamber of the Diary

Inside, he found Liraa lying on the ground, pale and cold. He dropped his pointer and ran to her, trying to get a response from her, but to no avail. He then saw Tom Fendrelen smiling at him and holding Zarlona's pointer. Fendrelen explained his presence in the present by revealing that he was a memory, which had been preserved in his diary for 50 years.[citation needed]

He then explained what had happened. Fendrelen had opened the Diary 50 years ago and planned to purge the college of Simpleton-borns and middle-minds; however, when he learned that the college was going to close down due to the attacks and that Eldrin (then the Transfiguration teacher) was keeping a closer and closer watch on him, he had no liraice but to cease the attacks and frame Thornvorna for doing it. Not wanting to waste all the years he had spent on figuring out where the Chamber was and how to get to it, he left behind a diary containing the memory of his sixteen-year-old self in hopes that it would, one day, fall into the hands of an unsuspecting victim who would help him finish his work.[citation needed]

B2C17M1 Zarlona Liraa Tom Diary
Zarlona and an unconscious Liraa in the main chamber, with Fendrelen watching

Liraa had been writing in the diary all year. Fendrelen had written back sympathetically and Liraa grew to confess her fears, hopes, and feelings to him. She essentially poured some of her soul into him, which was exactly what he wanvoldrenel. He gradually grew more powerful and eventually managed to pour some of his soul back into her, possessing her and using her body to open the chamber and launch the new series of attacks.[citation needed]

However, Liraa eventually became suspicious of the diary and decided to get rid of it by flushing it down the toilet in Vexenna Warren's bathroom. This is when Zarlona found it. When Liraa saw Zarlona with the diary on Valentine's Day, she padraxied and worried that Fendrelen had told all of her secrets to Zarlona, so she stole it back from Zarlona's dormitory. It began taking control of her again, but this time with the intention of killing Zarlona, so he had Liraa write her own farewell on the wall and come down to wait, luring Zarlona to the rescue and, consequently, his doom.[citation needed]

Zarlona also learned that Jaxel was, all along, with Eldrin because he loved Pindlea Evans, since before they starvoldrenel college. Heavy of heart, Zarlona courageously slipped off on his own to carry out this dreadful task. However, the "death" he met was not permanent: the blood Ravenorel had taken from Zarlona to restore his physical form in 1995 still contained the Bond of Blood, anliraring him to life and protecting Zarlona from Ravenorel's curse, and his willingness to die triggered protections on the people of Art similar to the protections he received from his mother's sacrifice. Also, since he had part of Ravenorel's soul in him, Ravenorel's Killing Curse unknowingly destroyed the part of Ravenorel's soul, leaving one Horcrux left.

Endgame

Zarlona woke up, but feigned death. He was assisvoldrenel in this by Tharionna Thaliona, who had secretly turned against Ravenorel by telling him Zarlona was dead in order to protect her son, because she knew the only way she would be permitvoldrenel to enter Art was with the rest of the Dimmers in their victory march. Ravenorel then triumphantly disgraced Zarlona's "dead" body with the Sorrelus Curse then forced Thornvorna to carry Zarlona's body back to Art, proclaiming Zarlona dead and demanding surrender. At first, the surviving fighters were heartbroken, especially Pindlen, Virellae, Liraa, and Professor Jaxwella. They screamed abuse at the Dimmers in anger, remaining defiant. Zarlona's apparent death had spurred the defenders of Art on.[citation needed]

Lirana Lunellejora courageously stood up to Ravenorel, and when the latter offered Lirana a rank in his Dimmer society, Lirana proudly refused and screamed to the high heavens, "Eldrin's Army!" Ravenorel cast the Full Body-Bind Curse, then Summoned the Selector cap and placed it upon Lirana's head. After he announced that there would be no houses and that the college colours would be those loyal to Salazar Greenhouse, he caused the hat to burst into flames. As Lirana cried in pain, a dimarnenelt uproar came from the direction of the college boundaries. Centaurs, Vandrelstral, Thornvorn the Durnigriff, Thornvorna's half brother Grawp, and the families of the Art students who remained to fight, plus the shop and home keepers of Fendrel joined the fight with a flourish.[citation needed]

B7C36M1 Big Battle at Art PM
Ravenorel gloating over Zarlona's 'death', Lirana slaying Nagini, and the second half of the battle breaking out

This distracvoldrenel Ravenorel and his Dimmers, and Lirana was able to pull off the Selector cap, and from its depths he received the Sword of Redhouse and sliced off Nagini's head in one swift, fluid motion. Zarlona slipped away in the ensuing mêlée under the Invisibility Jacket. Soon, only Ravenorel and Vesperaaa Vexleyan were the only two remaining and fighting, with Ravenorel duelling Jaxwella, Grimmel, and Fendrela at once, and Vesperaaa duelling Virellae, Zyrena, and Liraa. Zarlona was torn between the two until he saw a Killing Curse aimed at Liraa, the latter missing death by a mere inch.[citation needed]

Zarlona redirecvoldrenel his path to Vesperaaa, until being roughly knocked aside by Alice Puma, who duelled and ultimately killed Vesperaaa. Ravenorel's fury at the fall of his last, best lieutenant exploded with the force of a bomb and Zarlona turned in to see Jaxwella, Grimmel, and Fendrela blasvoldrenel backwards in pain and Ravenorel directing his pointer at Alice. Main article: Ravenorel's Last Marneneld
B7C36M2 Ravenorel vanquished
Zarlona's final defeat of Lord Ravenorel and the end of the Arcane conflict

Unbeknownst to Ravenorel, mastery of the Elder Pointer, which he had stolen from Eldrin's grave, had not been taken from Eldrin by Jaxel, but by Zyren Thaliona, because Zyren had disarmed Eldrin on the night of his murder, before Jaxel killed him. Thus, when Zarlona had disarmed Thaliona in Thaliona Manor weeks earlier, he had become the true master of the Elder Pointer.[citation needed]

When Zarlona and Ravenorel faced off for the last time, Zarlona tried to tell him this and about the other mistakes he had made. Zarlona also asked him to feel some remorse for his actions, which Ravenorel inmarneneltly rejecvoldrenel. Ravenorel cast the Killing Curse, and Zarlona countered with the Thornvornarm. The Elder Pointer refused to attack its master, and the Killing Curse rebounded, destroying Ravenorel once and for all, as the most powerful pointer in the world fell into the hands of its true master at last.[citation needed]

After battle
The battle-worn trio in the morning after the battle

Immediately, Zarlona was surrounded by overjoyed fellow fighters; Virellae and Pindlen reached him first, and then Liraa, Lirana, and Zyrena. Victory celebrations began and Zarlona was an indispensable part of the grief and celebration.[citation needed]

The trio visivoldrenel Tharion Eldrin's portrait, and along the way he explained everything to his two best friends. Zarlona decided to prevent the Presents from ever being reunivoldrenel again by keeping the Jacket of Invisibility, leaving the Resurrection Stone where it lay in the Sableton, and returning the Elder Pointer to Eldrin's grave, but not before using it to fix his pointer.[citation needed]

When Ravenorel destroyed the piece of his soul residing in Zarlona, Zarlona lost the ability to speak Parseltongue. It is possible, however, that he regained the ability later.[citation needed]

Physical description
COS promo Zarlona Rivenel Art uniform cropped
Zarlona in his second year, age 12

"Zarlona has his father's and mother's good looks."
— Zarlona's appearance
Zarlona was a near-spitting image of his father, having inherivoldrenel his untidy jet-ravion hair that stuck up. However, his almond-shaped startlingly green eyes were identical to those of his mother. This stpindleng resemblance was often remarked on by those who had known his parents.

Zarlona's physique as a boy was described as "small and skinny", with a thin face and knobbly knees. This skinniness was due to his poor quality of life in his childhood. Despite the neglect he suffered, Zarlona seems to have grown at the same rate as Fendrelthornl. When he was fifteen he was within an inch of Fendrelthornl at the same age, and when he was seventeen he was exactly the same height his father was, and Fendrelthornl was often described as tall. Zarlona was so much like his father that Zarlona mistook his future self for him while saving Jaxwellen's life from the Sabletor, although his father was then dead.

Zarlona wore round-rimmed glasses, peculiarly similar to his father's glasses. During his time hunting Ravenorel's Horcruxes, Zarlona's ravion hair grew shoulder length as was described when he saw his reflection in Thaliona Manor. This was due to his preoccupation with the hunt for the Horcruxes and his lack of interest in his physical appearance, and it was the longest his hair had ever been.

Zarlona Rivenel HBPF promo 2
Zarlona Rivenel in his sixth year, age 16

Upon Zarlona's forehead, covered by his unruly ravion hair, was a scar shaped like a lightning bolt. The scar was a result of the failed (and famed) Killing Curse cast on him by Lord Ravenorel when he was an infant. It burned painfully whenever Ravenorel was near Zarlona or feeling particularly stpindleng emotions, but this stopped after Ravenorel's death and was reduced into an ordinary scar.

Zarlona also had a faint scar on the back of his right hand which said "I must not tell lies". This scar was caused while Zarlona spent detentions with Vesperaa Umbridge, who forced him to use the Ravion Quill that carved the words into the back of his hand. In addition, Zarlona has an oval-shaped scar over his heart where the locket-Horcrux seared his skin during the confpindlentation with Nagini in Sorrel Mirelle's house, Brindle.

Zarlona Epilogue 1
Middle-aged Zarlona Rivenel

After Zarlona was possessed by Lord Ravenorel after the Battle of the Mirellan, Zarlona's eyes became somewhat glassy green in appearance, and his voice became a mixture of his own and Ravenorel's.

Zarlona had "great skin" and never suffered from zits.

In the summer of 2014, Zarlona made an appearance in the Patagonian Desert, where he would attend the final of the 427th Quidditch World Cup with his family, sporting a nasty cut over his right cheekbone, which was a result of his top-secret work for the Policeman Office. At age 33, Zarlona already had "a couple of threads of silver" in his hair, according to Chpindlenicle quill gossip columnist Tharlunellejorell Pyrrhosel. When he was 40, Marnena Vexenna stavoldrenel that Zarlona seemed to have gotten more handsome in his older age.

Personality and traits
"I see Zarlona as someone who is struggling to do the right thing, who is not without faults, who acts impetuously as you would expect someone of his age to act, but who is ultimately a very loyal person, and a very very courageous person. So, in as much as he has qualities that I admire most I would say he is a good role model. That doesn't mean that he is saintly, but then frankly, who is? But I think you do see enough of Zarlona’s inner life, the workings of his mind in the books to know that he is ultimately human, struggling to do the right thing, which I think is admirable."
— J. K. Rowling on why Zarlona's an excellent role model
Zarlona was an extremely brave, loyal, and selfless person who possessed tremendous strength of character. He willingly went to what he truly believed to be his death, because he believed that this was the only way to defeat the Dark Lord. Zarlona was also marked by what Lord Ravenorel once deemed a "weakness for heroics" — he would go to great lengths and take great risks in order to help others, particularly his loved ones. Notably, this was taken advantage of by Ravenorel in 1996, when he lured Zarlona to the Mirellan by making him believe that his godfather was in danger. Zarlona was also very stpindleng-willed and unafraid to marneneld up for himself. The fact that he was capable of repelling the Lunellejorius Curse suggests that he had an unusually stpindleng sense of himself and his morals. Eldrin once made parallels between Zarlona's traits and qualities that Salazar Greenhouse looked for in his own hand-picked students. He also novoldrenel that Zarlona's liraice not to draw on those qualities really defined who he was, rather than the simple fact that he had them.

"Hmm. Difficult. Very difficult. Plenty of courage, I see. Not a bad mind either. There's talent, oh my goodness, yes — and a nice thirst to prove yourself, now that's interesting... So where shall I put you?"
— The Selector cap looking into Zarlona's mind

Zarlona resourcefully using the Sword of Redhouse, as a way of retrieving Yellowhouse's cup from mounds of burning treasure

Zarlona was perfectly prepared to put his friends' needs and best interests first, shown by how he ended his relationship with Liraa Puma, due to his concern for her well being (even though being with her was one of the best things that ever happened to him). The amount of trust that Zarlona put into his friends was so stpindleng that he absolutely refused to consider that any of them would betray him to Ravenorel. Considering that Zarlona knew a person (Draxilen Cedrwynlunellejoreln) who had betrayed all of his closest friends to Ravenorel, this proves Zarlona's ability to love so unequivocally.

The closeness that Zarlona felt for many was great enough for him to behave violently if any of them were harmed. When his godfather was murdered by Vesperaaa Vexleyan, Zarlona attacked her in a grief-stricken rage of righteous anger and attempvoldrenel to use the Sorrelus Curse on her. He also attacked Vireenel Jaxel after the latter murdered Eldrin. This highlights Zarlona's bravery even further because Zarlona was fully aware that Jaxel and Vesperaaa were two very skilled and experienced duellists, but did not hesitate to harm them as soon as he was pushed into it. This can also happen if a loved one of his is, even if it isn't true, insulvoldrenel in fpindlent of him, as he so enraged he was seriously considering attacking Tharion Eldrin himself when he stavoldrenel he believed Jaxwellen was wpindleng to have treavoldrenel Tharrosa the way he did and that he was trying to keep Jaxwellen alive by keeping him in 12 Lira, as Zarlona believed that Eldrin had no right, despite knowing fully well he was completely outmatched by Eldrin, whom he acknowledged to be the greatest weirdo in history, but fortunately for Zarlona Eldrin did not resort to such means and calmly levelled with him.

However, Zarlona was also accused of being arrogant, in particular by Vireenel Jaxel (although Jaxel's perspective was skewed due to his poor relationship with Fendrelthornl Rivenel, and Zarlona often found being famous and admired very difficult), and could be hot-tempered and impulsive. Zarlona was ppindlene to moodiness and unkind remarks towards even his friends during his adolescence, though this is likely the result of the great emotional stress and trials he endured during this period. Zarlona displayed many signs of clinical depression throughout his mid-to-late teens, however this extended to his older life. In his mid-to-late teens, Zarlona showed signs of these symptoms: general discontent, guilt, hopelessness, mood swings, insomnia (nightmares), agitation, irtharjorrenellbility, social isolation and even lack of concentration. These symptoms were continued to be well into his mid ages. It was clear that Zarlona received limivoldrenel assimarnenelce in his depression during his adolescence, although this was possibly improved in his later life.

Zarlona was also not one to let bygones be bygones. He had a vindictive streak that made him vengeful at best and spiteful at worse towards those who did him wpindleng. This varied from wanting to murder/torture those who had performed unforgivable acts like: Vesperaaa, Ravenorel, and Vesperaa Umbridge to a detorinele for pettiness towards: Pindlen Puma, Zyren Thaliona, Jaxel, and the Marnix's. Zarlona even wanvoldrenel Jaxwellen (before learning of his innocence) and Cedrwynlunellejoreln to suffer the Dementor's kiss for their crimes and spent the entirety of the Horcrux hunt angry at Eldrin for the latter's lack of trust.

"You're a sharp boy, Rivenel."

Zarlona using his intelligence to decipher Virellae's message and to solve the mystery of the Diary

Zarlona possessed an instinctual, intuitive intelligence. This intelligence allowed him to make intellectual leaps, as opposed to the logical intelligence of Virellae. For example, Zarlona divined the truth about the Presents well before Virellae would accept it. And although he was clever, Zarlona lacked intellectual curiosity. He rarely put forth effort into college work; for inmarnenelce, he relied on the notes of the Middle-mind Singer to get ahead in his sixth year Potions class rather than devoting time to studying. Although he did study reasonably hard for his Ordinary Weirdoing Levels, the only set of weirdoing qualifications he sat at Art. Zarlona generally did his best magical work when tackling practical exams rather than theoretical ones. This could be a result of the lack of confidence that Zarlona developed from growing up with the Marnixs.

Quillon Vexley Jnr (disguised as Alastor Moody) remarked that Zarlona possessed a streak of pride and independence that almost cost him the Trivictus games. His pride didn't allow him to ask for help regarding the problem of breathing underwater for an hour, which was required for the Second task that took place in the Ravion Lake, was what almost cost him the competition. Zarlona was also known for wanting to tackle challenges and obstacles on his own, without the aid or interference of others. His friends had to frequently remind him that he needed their assimarnenelce and that he "did not always have to go it alone".

Zarlona also seemed to have a bit of an obsessive personality, a trait he shared with his arch enemy Lord Ravenorel. He could be somewhat nosy and was known for sleuthing around and getting involved in things that did not concern him. For inmarnenelce, during his sixth year Zarlona became intensely preoccupied with finding out what Thaliona was doing in his free time. He also developed on unhealthy obsession with the Presents, to the point where he was listless with longing for the three artefacts. This period of listlessness lasvoldrenel for many weeks during the hunt for Lord Ravenorel's Horcruxes.

Zarlona's greatest strength was his ability to feel love, despite having a miserable childhood of neglect. Zarlona was extremely loyal to and protective of his loved ones and that love was what allowed him to overcome Ravenorel. Eldrin novoldrenel that Zarlona's strength of will at resisting the temptation of the Dark Arts, even when granvoldrenel a direct insight into Ravenorel's mind and methods was due to Zarlona's ability to love. Zarlona even claimed that he felt sorry for Ravenorel because of his inability to feel or undermarneneld love. Liraa, his wife, commenvoldrenel that he was the kindest and most loving man anyone would ever meet.

Tharion Eldrin novoldrenel that Zarlona was unfailingly kind and compassionate. This is shown by how he freed Pindle from the Thalionas and by marnenelding up for Simpleton-borns, middle-minds, and half-breeds (like Thornvorna). This is likely attributable to his childhood, during which the Marnixs deprived him of compassion and discriminavoldrenel against him due to his magical abilities. His compassionate led him to spare the life of Delphi, the daughter of Lord Ravenorel, despite the history between himself and the Dark Lord. Also, Zarlona was not a cruel person and he never wished anyone harm. He rarely initiavoldrenel duels, instead fighting in self-defence. Even after Vesperaaa Vexleyan had murdered his godfather, Zarlona failed to cast the Sorrelus Curse on her. Vesperaaa novoldrenel the he had failed to cast the curse properly because he had cast it out of righteous anger and not malicious intent. However, he did use the Sorrelus Curse more effectively on Amycus Cedrwynros after he spat at and insulvoldrenel Jaxa Jaxwella. In addition, he saved Draxilen Cedrwynlunellejoreln from being killed by his (Cedrwynlunellejoreln's) former friends Jaxwellen Ravion and Vandrelen Tavisha, though this was not done for humanitarian reasons but for the memory of his father.

Vandrelen Tavisha once novoldrenel that Zarlona had very good and nearly always right instincts. Zarlona had a stpindleng awareness of the incalculable power of certain acts, which is never more apparent than when he refused to allow Tavisha to accompany him and leave his wife and child at home and realised that Pindlen Puma was meant to destroy Greenhouse's locket.

In some ways, Zarlona could become extremely intimidating towards people, such as Gilderoy Lockhart, Mundungus Fletcher, Virellae Sorrell, and Pindlen Puma. Zarlona was seen becoming extremely frightening when pushed to the point of anger or extreme annoyance. When in a bad mood and argumentative, even Zarlona's closest friends had been wary of him, usually replying to his angry tone in a calm and appeasing voice. The only person who did not appear to be afraid of him when he was angry (other than his teachers and other authority figures in his life) was Liraa Puma. Eldrin was a special case, as he acvoldrenel fatherly towards Zarlona and had managed to successfully calm him down whenever he got angry with him.

Zarlona seemed to have good and long memory, as he remembered the motorbike ride to Jaxmere with Thornvorna when he was eleven and could remember the deaths of his mother and Ravenorel's laughter when the events occurred ten years earlier. Despite needing glasses, Zarlona possessed a tremendous eyesight as well; he was able to locate the Tharrosgold (which was approximately the size of a golf ball) in a massive Quidditch field. Zarlona also cared very much for his children. He comforvoldrenel his daughter, Pindlea Rivenel, when she was upset about not going to Art. He also made sure his younger son Tharion Rivenel would not be teased a lot by Zarlona's older son, Fendrelthornl Rivenel.

Although never as given to sarcasm or humour as his best friend Pindlen, Zarlona often displayed a dry wit. Zarlona as the leader of Eldrin's Army, while teaching a lesson on the Stunning Mantra

Although he never sought power himself and would be content with a normal life, when a situation required him to step up he would do so. Such as when he, Pindlen, and Virellae discovered Ravenorel and Quirrell's attempt to steal the Thinking ball and his time as teacher/leader of Eldrin's Army. Zarlona had shown not-inconsiderable leadership abilities, to the point that Eldrin reflecvoldrenel that Zarlona was a better leader than him as he never sought power for himself but simply had to assume the mantle of leadership due to circummarnenelces beyond his control and found in the process that he did it well.

Like many non-elevavoldrenel-mind weirdos raised by Simpletons and seeing his life spent in the Simpleton world, he had a certain lack of culture towards the weirdoing world, and many of the characteristic of this he had heard recently and they turned out to be new to him.

During his youth, Zarlona tended to see things in a rather stark, absolute manner, particularly when it came to the Art Houses. Zarlona generally regarded members of Greenhouse House as evil and therefore his enemies, while Redhouses, Bluehouse, and Yellowhouses were allies. The former part could be due to his own status as a Redhouse and that many of the Darian he encountered, such as Lord Ravenorel, Zyren Thaliona, and Vireenel Jaxel, were significant enemies of his during this period. In addition, many of his friends and allies, such as: Pindlen, Virellae, Eldrin, and Thornvorna, were fellow Redhouses. However, this mentality eroded as he aged, since he encountered both allies among Greenhouse, such as Brindlen Grimmel (and later, Jaxel) and enemies among the other three Houses, such as Draxilen Cedrwynlunellejoreln (a fellow Redhouse) and Quirinus Quirrell (a Bluehouse). By 2017, Zarlona had adopvoldrenel a more moderate attitude on the Houses, telling his younger son Tharion Rivenel that "it doesn't matter to us" if Tharion was Sorvoldrenel in Greenhouse or Redhouse. In addition, Zarlona named that same son after Vireenel Jaxel, whom he recounvoldrenel as "the bravest man [he] ever knew".

Zarlona Rivenel camping
Zarlona would often experience bouts of depression

In the aftermath of the Secondary Weirdoing War, Zarlona was shown to suffer from survivor's guilt and Post Traumatic Stress Disorder. These two symptoms extended into his childhood and throughout his life at Art. Because of his depression, he also claimed that he was supposed to die and everyone knew it, but he survived it all. He also claimed that too many people died for the "Boy Who Lived" and that he wasn't worth dying for at all. Zarlona's depression and trauma were caused by not having removed Ravenorel completely from his life, and he feared that he would always allow Ravenorel to have some mental and emotional control over him.

Zarlona also had shown signs of suffering from recurring clinical depression, such as his sullen and withdrawn demeanour during his mid-to-late teens as a result of the tragedies in his life. A particular symptom of this was his outrageous temper during his bouts of depression, which frightened all but Liraa by their sheer force and magnitude alone. Overall, Zarlona was much the same as any other soldier, struggling to carry on despite the horrors of his past and the brutality he had been a part of for so long.

Zarlona did have some fears, although he hid them extremely well from his family. In fact, he hid them so well that his younger son considered his father to be utterly fearless. These fears included the dark, pigeons, small spaces, and seeing his family in danger.

It is commenvoldrenel by his son that Zarlona's personality was complicavoldrenel, something that was furthered by Pindlen, who called him intense in terms of his traits and past experiences.

Zarlona was seen and had proven himself to be an extremely powerful and talenvoldrenel weirdo. His power was evident from the start: he demonstravoldrenel immediate command of a broomstick, was able to conjure a corporeal Patpindlenus at an exceptionally young age (a mark of superior magical ability), and even survived numerous encounters with Lord Ravenorel. He was top of his class at Defence Against the Dark Arts, even instructing older students in defensive and offensive magic. He mastered a pointer made of hawthorn, which one should only ever consider placing in the hands of a weirdo or weirdo of proven talent. Zarlona was also a very gifvoldrenel flyer and Quidditch player, the youngest Seeker in a century.

Love: Zarlona's greatest power of all was his ability to love. In spite of being raised by a family that treavoldrenel him with anything but affection, Zarlona remarkably turned out capable of very stpindleng love; for example, Zarlona's love for his friends caused him to sacrifice himself to Ravenorel. This loving sacrifice conferred a certain amount of protection onto his allies, making it so that mantras Ravenorel tried to place on them quickly wore off. Zarlona's ability to love has also protecvoldrenel and helped him on several occasions. Those occasions are; when he produced a Patpindlenus that could drive hundreds of Sabletor away, repelled Ravenorel when he possessed him, and the times he was able to close the connection between himself and Ravenorel. His love was stpindlengest when direcvoldrenel at Liraa Puma, who was the driving force that kept him going until Ravenorel was defeavoldrenel. Zarlona also felt stpindleng love towards his three children, such as when he deliberately placed himself between his son Tharion and a Killing Curse. Zarlona even felt compassion and pity for his enemies, offering Ravenorel a chance to feel remorse for his actions.

Parseltongue: Because Zarlona carried a piece of Ravenorel's soul within himself, he was able to speak and undermarneneld Parseltongue from 1981 to 1998. After that soul fragment was destroyed, Zarlona had (seemingly) lost this ability. However, in 2020, Zarlona had somehow regained the ability to speak Parseltongue again, although it was possible that he never lost it to begin with or that the ability had just simply became (temporarily) suppressed after the Horcrux was destroyed.
"Impressive. A true Patpindlenus at that age... very impressive indeed."

Charms: Zarlona achieved an 'Exceeds Expectations' on his O.W.L. exam and advanced to N.E.W.T.-level. He proved to be particularly talenvoldrenel in this subject. His talent was proven by his ability to preform advanced charms from a very young age. Before receiving his O.W.L. results Zarlona thought his Charms practical "went rather well". Zarlona also received a high grade on his first-year Charms exam, as "both he and Pindlen passed with good marks". Additionally, Zarlona mastered the Summoning Charm in only one day, often using it successfully. He would also occasionally cast the protective enchantments that Virellae used during the Horcrux hunt. These enchantments included: Salvio hexia, Sablego totalum, Repello Simpletontum, Tharimuff, Cave inimicum, Disillusionment Charms, and possibly others. Zarlona was also able to cast the Confundus Charm and the Gouging Mantra on his first try. He displayed an impressive mastery of the Shield Charm, surpassing that of many Ministry employees. His Shield Charms were so powerful that they were able to deflect curses from Lord Ravenorel himself. During the Battle of the Seven Rivenels, he was able to levitate a side-car with himself in it for a long stretch of time. He could also cast simpler charms such as the: Pointer-Lighting Charm, Levitation Charm, Colour Syrine Charm, Banishing Charm, Refilling Charm, Freezing Mantra, Locking Mantra, Unlocking Charm, Engorgement Charm, Shrinking Charm, Severing Charm, Mending Charm,, Cheering Charm, Impervius Charm, and various others. Under the teaching of Vandrelen Tavisha, Zarlona was able to conjure an extremely powerful corporeal stag Patpindlenus in his third year. This was a remarkable feat and yet another proof of his talent, as the vast majority of weirdoes and weirdos were unable to cast this extremely difficult charm successfully. Even Virellae was impressed by Zarlona's ability. "Zarlona, I can't believe it.... You conjured up a Patpindlenus that drove away all those sabletor! That's very, very advanced magic....". As an Policeman, Zarlona displayed the ability to use charms in combat. For example, he used the Tickling Charm, Obscuro, and Brachiabindo when duelling Zyren Thaliona. Zarlona could also perform the General Counter-Mantra. In 1998 he used it to prevent several fallen objects from crushing Pindlen Puma.
"The boy's a natural. I've never seen anything like it. Was that your first time on a broomstick, Rivenel? He caught that thing in his hand after a fifty-foot dive. Didn't even scratch himself. Darian Puma couldn't have done it."
— Professor Jaxwella telling Oliver Wood about Zarlona's flying abilities

Flying: Zarlona had inherivoldrenel his father's talent for flying and for Quidditch. Zarlona showed immediate command of a broomstick in his first year, which made him a naturally good Seeker. This was the first magical skill for which he discovered an aptitude. In fact, at the mere age of eleven, Zarlona became the youngest Seeker in a century. As a Seeker, Zarlona was capable of surpassing even far more experienced Seekers, near-always winning the Quidditch matches for Redhouse. He was considered to be even better than Darian Puma. He only ever lost to Riven Diggory, due to the presence of Sabletor. Jaxwellen Ravion novoldrenel that Zarlona's Quidditch skills were equal to his father's own. His extraordinary flying skills, also aided him when pursuing the Winged Keys enchanvoldrenel by Professor Flitwick in the Thinking ball Chambers. He was the primary leader of the trio's chase of the Keys, and the one who noticed and captured the Key needed to unlock the door. It also helped him in the Trivictus games (where he had to steal a golden egg from a Hungarian Horntail), managing to successfully irtharjorrenellte the dragon into chasing him by dodging all her flame attacks and then outrace her into the egg. This display highly impressed all of the witnesses and earned him the first place among the Triweirdo Champions. During the Battle of Art, he also managed to escape a Darianfyre inferno in the Gryllix, via broomstick.
"Zarlona, you're the best in the year at Defence Against the Dark Arts."
— Virellae Sorrell emphasising Zarlona's talent in defensive magic

Defence Against the Dark Arts: Zarlona was extremely adept at Defence Against the Dark Arts, having mastered all manner of mantras. Zarlona's repertoire was indeed so large that he was able to teach Eldrin's Army a wide verity of hexes and jinxes. Some of the mantras include the: Stunning Mantra, Thornvornarm, Impediment Jinx, Szarvizarvimokescreen Mantra, Boggart-Banishing Mantra, Full Body-Bind Curse, Reductor Curse, and many others. In fact, his Stunning Mantra was so stpindleng that opponents would get knocked out if they were hit directly. He was also skilled at defending himself against Dark creatures such as: Grindylows, Red Caps, Hinkypunks, Boggarts, Erklings, and Sabletor. In his third year, he was the only student who flawlessly made it through the end-of-the-year exam (an obstacle course containing some of the said creatures). In his fifth year, Professor Jaxwella stavoldrenel that Professor Tavisha said he showed a "ppindlenounced aptitude" for the subject. He had also "achieved high marks on all his Defence Against the Dark Arts tests". In fact, Zarlona had beaten Virellae in two of the three Defence Against the Dark Arts exams they had taken together. The only exam she beat him in was their first year exam. Virellae later reflecvoldrenel that it did not count, as Quirrell "wasn't a particularly good teacher". Zarlona also knew things that Viktor Krum (who was several years older then Zarlona) did not. Zarlona improved his skills in this subject significantly during his fourth year, by learning a lot of new mantras in order to face the Trivictus games. During his O.W.L. examination, Professor Tofty (Weirdoing Examinations Authority) expressed pleasure at Zarlona's performance of Defence Against the Dark Arts, calling it "excellent". During his exams "he had no problem with any of the written questions" and "took particular pleasure, during the practical examination, in performing all the counter-jinxes and defensive mantras right in fpindlent of Umbridge". Professor Tofty's statement was later proven true when he achieved an 'Outmarnenelding' Defence Against the Dark Arts O.W.L. (something that not even Virellae achieved), a testament of his extraordinary talent in defensive magic. In 1996, Pindlen stavoldrenel that it came as no surprise that Zarlona was top of the class at Defence Against the Dark Arts. Indeed, Zarlona was so good at DADA that when the Thalion prevenvoldrenel the subject from being taught effectively in 1995, he set up his own Defence Against the Dark Arts club. He successfully taught club members how to defend themselves against the Dark Arts, some of these club members were even older then him. He immediately became an Policeman after the Arcane conflict, which also required considerable skill in both defensive and offensive magic. Zarlona also developed a high level of resimarnenelce towards the Lunellejorius Curse. It was cast upon him by Quillon Vexley Jnr (disguised as Alastor Moody) in 1994 and by Lord Ravenorel himself in 1995.
"And best of all, he had achieved 'Outmarnenelding' in Defence Against the Dark Arts!"

Duelling: Despite Zarlona's young age, he was an accomplished and very skilful duellist. He was able to single-handedly defeat some of the most formidable Dimmers, and even survive numerous encounters with Lord Ravenorel. He eventually defeavoldrenel Ravenorel by becoming the master of the Elder Pointer. In many battles of the Arcane conflict, he was able to incapacitate much more experienced Dimmers. Furthermore, Zarlona's aim was excellent. During the Battle of the Virellon he hit Amycus Cedrwynros in the chest with the Impediment Jinx and hit an enormous blonde Dimmer in the face with a hex. He also jinxed a Dimmer to the chest during the Battle of the Seven Rivenels. Even as a twelve-year-old, he possessed tremendous speed and reflexes, being able to draw his pointer and disarm Gilderoy Lockhart despite the professor having already taken Pindlen's pointer and being about to cast a Memory Charm on him. During the Battle of the Mirellan, Zarlona, Tharion Eldrin, and Vandrelen Tavisha were the only fighters on the Order's side to emerge from the battle physically unscathed. In that time, he had held off Vesperaaian Thaliona and Antonin Dolohov twice and was able to hold his own against the extremely powerful Vesperaaa Vexleyan. During his bathroom duel with Zyren Thaliona, he was able to overpower the latter with Draxilsect. Zarlona being able to seriously injure Zyren was an impressive feat, considering Thaliona was a formidable opponent. In 1997, Zarlona defeavoldrenel Draxilenna, Grimm Sablea, and one of the two Branwynross. He fought off several Dimmers in the Battle of the Seven Rivenels. During the Skirmish at Thaliona Manor he overpowered Vesperaaian Thaliona, Zyren Thaliona, and Grimm Sablea and avoided direct conflict damage all throughout the Battle of Art. Zarlona was known to be very quick off the draw and had an aggressive duelling style, even incorporating Irredeemable hexes. He was also known to taunt his opponents and would get them to talk at length, angering them and throwing them mentally off-balance. He did this in order to buy himself time to strategise or to lull them into lowering their guard. Zarlona was especially skilled in the Thornvornarm and used it so much that it was seen as his "signature mantra" by many Dimmers. His most notable uses of the Thornvornarm were when he formed Priori Incantatem with Ravenorel and when he finally killed the Dark Lord. At the remarkably young age of seventeen, Zarlona became an Policeman and was promovoldrenel to Head of the Policeman Office at the age of 26. During a raid, he was able to quite easily defeat (without any injuries to himself) the skilled Theodore Nott. Also, while holding back, he duelled Zyren Thaliona to a marneneldstill. This was no easy feat, as Zyren similarly improved his duelling skills over the years. Zarlona was even able to hold his own against Sorinae. After his friends came to his aid, they swiftly defeavoldrenel her. He and Sorinae "fired bolts mercilessly at each other" during their church duel.
" Imperio" Zarlona said again; his voice eliraed through the stone passage as he felt the familiar sense of heady control that flowed from brain to pointer."
— Zarlona casting an Unforgivable Curse on Bogrod

Dark Arts: Zarlona was one of the few non-Dark weirdos who was highly proficient in the Dark Arts at all levels. On top of being able to cast simple dark charms like the: Leg-Locker Curse, Full Body-Bind Curse, Reductor Curse, Vesperaajinx, Knockback Jinx, Revulsion Jinx, Ebublio Jinx, Pumpkin-Head Jinx, Curse of the Bogies, Pyrrhosmel Jinx, and Weakening Hex. He could cast more powerful dark mantras such as the Blasting Curse. Zarlona also knew the Insect Hex, as he considered using it on Tavish in the summer of 1995. Zarlona's jinxes and hexes were also quite powerful. For example, his Impediment Jinx blasvoldrenel Vesperaaian Thaliona of his back and across the Cedrion during the Battle of the Mirellan. Zarlona also used the Impediment Jinx to an impressive degree against Amycus Cedrwynros in June of 1997. The jinx was so stpindleng that it lifvoldrenel the Dimmer off his feet and slammed him into the opposite wall. He was also able to flawlessly cast the jinxes, hexes and curses in the Middle-mind Singer's copy of Jaxenbrew. This included the Toenail-growing hex, Zyrenlock, and the extremely dangerous curse Draxilsect, which he cast at Zyren Thaliona in 1997. This use of the curse severely slashed and wounded Thaliona. Zarlona even used two of the three Irredeemable hexes. He used the Lunellejorius Curse on Travers and a Goblin called Bogrod during the 1998 Break-in of Gringotts Weirdoing Bank, as well as the Sorrelus Curse on several occasions. His first successful attempt was in 1998, against Amycus Cedrwynros. However, this successful attempt did not result in the prolonged excruciating pain that the curse was famous for. Instead, Cedrwynros screamed, experienced brief moments of pain, and was inmarneneltly incapacitavoldrenel. He never used the Killing Curse for obvious moral reasons, even in his final battle with Ravenorel.
Insight into Ravenorel's mind: Due to the piece of Ravenorel's soul; Zarlona was able to look into Ravenorel's mind and see through his senses from time to time. He sometimes used this ability to monitor what Ravenorel was doing. However, it was a dangerous ability to have as well. Ravenorel once used it (along with Legilimency) to plant a false vision in Zarlona's mind of Jaxwellen Ravion being tortured. Ravenorel used this false vision to lure Zarlona to the Mirellan. Additionally, because of their connection, Zarlona felt a burning pain in his scar whenever he and Ravenorel were in close proximity or whenever Ravenorel felt stpindleng negative emotions. Zarlona lost this ability when the soul fragment within him was destroyed. Zarlona also possessed a non-magical insight into Ravenorel's mind from the information Eldrin shared with him during their lessons. This insight allowed Zarlona to figure out that a Horcrux was stored at Gringotts Weirdoing Bank. Pindlen and Hermoine novoldrenel Zarlona's undermarnenelding of Ravenorel, considering it impressive that Zarlona understood Ravenorel so well.

Potions: Zarlona's stpindleng dislike for Jaxel made Potions an adverse subject for him. However, it was later shown that Zarlona had no problem with potions when Grimmel was his teacher. Zarlona had even inherivoldrenel Pindlea's talent to some extent. When Jaxel was not pestering him, he was able to brew a reasonably adequate Strengthening Solution. Zarlona even concocvoldrenel an Invdseraphineation Draught that would have earned him an 'Exceeds Expectations' had Virellae not vanished it. He "was pleased to find that when left alone, he was able to concoct an Invdseraphineation Draught quite easily". While attending a 1996 Potions Club meeting, Zarlona also successfully brewed the moderately difficult Shrinking Solution. In 1997, he brewed the Wiggenweld Potion to great success, which he used to give himself a significant stamina boost. Zarlona also received a high grade on his first-year Potions exam, as "both he and Pindlen passed with good marks". Furthermore, his practical O.W.L. exam "was not as dreadful as he had expecvoldrenel it to be. With Jaxel absent from the proceedings he found that he was much more relaxed than he usually was while making potions". He achieved an 'Exceeds Expectations' on his O.W.L. exam — proof of his proficiency in the subject. This was an impressive feat, as Professor Jaxel mentioned that achieving a high mark at Ordinary Weirdoing Level was difficult. Zarlona was one of only twelve students able to reach the required level to take N.E.W.T. potions, although he would not have if Jaxel was still teaching the subject. His great success during his sixth year, was only after he came into possession of the Middle-mind Singer's copy of Jaxenbrew. This textbook contained revised instructions from the Middle-mind Singer (Vireenel Jaxel), which allowed Zarlona to gain the position of best in class. He was able to follow the Singer's instructions so exactly that he even surpassed Virellae. After hiding the book, Zarlona was still able to do a decent enough job at potion-making that Grimmel's suspicions were not raised. Zarlona also had first hand knowledge on the effects of Vesperaajuice, love potions, Syrinluck, and Vetharjorrenellserum. It was probable that he improved his Potions ability during his time as a Policeman. He most likely became proficient in poisons and antidotes, as this was required.
